<script type="text/javascript">
  
             YAHOO.util.Dom.setStyle('navbar_password_hint', "display", "none");
             YAHOO.util.Dom.setStyle('navbar_password', "display", "inline");
  
             vB_XHTML_Ready.subscribe(function()
             {
             //
                 YAHOO.util.Event.on('navbar_username', "focus", navbar_username_focus);
                 YAHOO.util.Event.on('navbar_username', "blur", navbar_username_blur);
                 YAHOO.util.Event.on('navbar_password_hint', "focus", navbar_password_hint);
                 YAHOO.util.Event.on('navbar_password', "blur", navbar_password);
             });
             
             function navbar_username_focus(e)
             {
             //
                 var textbox = YAHOO.util.Event.getTarget(e);
                 if (textbox.value == 'User Name')
                 {
                 //
                     textbox.value='';
                     textbox.style.color='#303030';
                 }
             }
 
             function navbar_username_blur(e)
             {
             //
                 var textbox = YAHOO.util.Event.getTarget(e);
                 if (textbox.value == '')
                 {
                 //
                     textbox.value='User Name';
                     textbox.style.color='#828282';
                 }
             }
             
             function navbar_password_hint(e)
             {
             //
                 var textbox = YAHOO.util.Event.getTarget(e);
                 
                 YAHOO.util.Dom.setStyle('navbar_password_hint', "display", "inlin");
                 YAHOO.util.Dom.setStyle('navbar_password', "display", "inline");
                 YAHOO.util.Dom.get('navbar_password').focus();
             }
 
             function navbar_password(e)
             {
             //
                 var textbox = YAHOO.util.Event.getTarget(e);
                 
                 if (textbox.value == '')
                 {
                     YAHOO.util.Dom.setStyle('navbar_password_hint', "display", "none");
                     YAHOO.util.Dom.setStyle('navbar_password', "display", "inline");
                 }
             }
             </script>

                 function initQuotes() {

                             '%)</span></div><svg id="' + String(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].name).toLowerCase() + 'Svg' + '" version="1.1" xmlns="https://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="https://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px"\n' +
                             '\t preserveAspectRatio="none"><defs><linearGradient id="gradient' + index + '" x1="0" x2="0" y1="0" y2="1"><stop class="stop1' + tmpIndex + '" offset="0%"/><stop class="stop2' + tmpIndex + '" offset="100%"/></linearGradient>\n' +
                             '<filter id="filter' + index + '"><feGaussianBlur in="FillPaint" stdDeviation="5" /><feOffset dx="0" dy="0" /><feMerge><feMergeNode /><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic" /></feMerge></filter>' +
                             '<linearGradient id="gradientLine' + index + '" x1="0" y1="0" x2="75" y2="0" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse" gradientTransform="rotate(90)"><stop stop-color="' + arrayColor[tmpIndex] + '" offset="0" stop-opacity="0.1" /><stop stop-color="' + arrayColor[tmpIndex] + '" offset="0.5" stop-opacity="1" /><stop stop-color="' + arrayColor[tmpIndex] + '" offset="1" stop-opacity="0.1" /></linearGradient>\n' +

                 function prepareGraph() {
                     clearInterval(intervalIdQuotesFromSocket);
                     var chartsWidgetBlockChildren = chartsWidgetBlock.children;
                     var chartsWidgetBlockChildrenSize = getSizeParam(chartsWidgetBlockChildren[0]);
                     svgWidth = chartsWidgetBlockChildrenSize.width;
                     svgHeight = chartsWidgetBlockChildrenSize.height - chartsWidgetBlockChildren[0].getElementsByTagName('DIV')[0].clientHeight;
 
                     widthPoint = Math.ceil(svgWidth / pointsOnGraph);
 
                     for (var i = 0, size = chartsWidgetBlockChildren.length; i < size; i++) {
                         checkReadyQuotes[chartsWidgetBlockChildren[i].id] = false;
                         getQuotesOneInfo(chartsWidgetBlockChildren[i], chartsWidgetBlockChildren[i].id);
                     }
 
                     var intervalId = setInterval(function () {
                         var checkAll = true;
 
                         Object.keys(checkReadyQuotes).map(function (quote, index) {
                             if (checkReadyQuotes[quote] === false) {
                                 checkAll = false;
                             }
                         });
 
                         if (checkAll) {
                             clearInterval(intervalId);
                             window.addEventListener("resize", resizeWindow);
                             setTimeout(function () {
                                 resizeWindow();
                                 chartsWidgetBlock.style.opacity = 1;
                                 /*getQuotesFromSocket();*/
                             }, 50);
                         }
                     }, 50);
                 }
 
                 function resizeWindow() {
                     var chartsWidgetBlockChildren = chartsWidgetBlock.children;
                     var chartsWidgetBlockStyle = getComputedStyle(chartsWidgetBlock);
                     var chartsWidgetBlockWidth = document.body.clientWidth - lengthMenu - parseInt(chartsWidgetBlockStyle.paddingLeft) - parseInt(chartsWidgetBlockStyle.paddingRight) - parseInt(chartsWidgetBlockStyle.marginLeft) - parseInt(chartsWidgetBlockStyle.marginRight);
                     var length = 0;
                     var chartsWidgetBlockChildrenSize = getSizeParam(chartsWidgetBlockChildren[0]);
                     svgWidth = chartsWidgetBlockChildrenSize.width;
                     svgHeight = chartsWidgetBlockChildrenSize.height - chartsWidgetBlockChildren[0].getElementsByTagName('DIV')[0].clientHeight;
                     widthPoint = Math.ceil(svgWidth / pointsOnGraph);
                     for (var i = 0, size = chartsWidgetBlockChildren.length; i < size; i++) {
                         var svg = chartsWidgetBlockChildren[i].getElementsByTagName('svg')[0];
                         svg.setAttribute('width', String(svgWidth));
                         svg.setAttribute('height', String(svgHeight));
                         svg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 ' + svgWidth + ' ' + svgHeight);
                         chartsWidgetBlockChildren[i].style.display = 'block';
                         length += chartsWidgetBlockChildren[i].clientWidth + chartsWidgetBlockChildrenSize.marginLeftRight;
                         if (length > chartsWidgetBlockWidth) {
                             chartsWidgetBlock.style.width = (length - chartsWidgetBlockChildren[i].clientWidth) + 'px';
                             for (var n = i; n < size; n++) {
                                 chartsWidgetBlockChildren[n].style.display = 'none';
                             }
                             break;
                         } else if (length <= chartsWidgetBlockWidth && i === size - 1) {
                             chartsWidgetBlock.style.width = length + 'px';
                         }
                     }
                 }
 
                 function getSizeParam(element) {
                     var style = element.currentStyle || window.getComputedStyle(element),
                         width = parseFloat(style.width),
                         height = parseFloat(style.height),
                         marginLeftRight = parseFloat(style.marginLeft) + parseFloat(style.marginRight),
                         paddingLeftRight = parseFloat(style.paddingLeft) + parseFloat(style.paddingRight),
                         borderLeftRight = parseFloat(style.borderLeftWidth) + parseFloat(style.borderRightWidth),
                         marginTopBottom = parseFloat(style.marginTop) + parseFloat(style.marginBottom),
                         paddingTopBottom = parseFloat(style.paddingTop) + parseFloat(style.paddingBottom),
                         borderTopBottom = parseFloat(style.borderTopWidth) + parseFloat(style.borderBottomWidth);
 
                     return ({
                         width: width,
                         height: height,
                         marginLeftRight: marginLeftRight,
                         paddingLeftRight: paddingLeftRight,
                         borderLeftRight: borderLeftRight,
                         marginTopBottom: marginTopBottom,
                         paddingTopBottom: paddingTopBottom,
                         borderTopBottom: borderTopBottom
                     });
                 }
 
                 function getQuotesOneInfo(target, symbol) {
                     var xmlHttp = getXmlHttp();
                     
 
                     xmlHttp.open("GET", "https://client-api.instaforex.com/api/chart/GetJsonCandles?symbol=" + encodeURIComponent(symbol.toUpperCase()) + "&type=M1&count=" + pointsOnGraph);
                     xmlHttp.timeout = 1000;
 
                     var intervalID = setInterval(function () {
                         if (xmlHttp.readyState === 4) {
                             if (xmlHttp.status === 200) {
                                 clearInterval(intervalID);
                                 arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'] = JSON.parse(xmlHttp.responseText);
                                 arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'] = [];
                                 arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['buffer']['change'] = 0;
                                 arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['buffer']['timestamp'] = 0;
 
                                 setQuotesOneInfo(target, symbol);
 
                                 target.onmousemove = function (e) {
                                     clearTimeout(timeoutIdToolTip);
                                     var target = e.target;
 
                                     while (target !== this) {
                                         if (target.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'svg' || target.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'span') {
                                             if (target.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'span') {
                                                 while (target.tagName.toLowerCase() !== 'div') {
                                                     target = target.parentNode;
                                                 }
 
                                                 target = target.getElementsByTagName('svg')[0];
                                             }
                                             if (typeof target === 'undefined') {
                                                 return;
                                             }
                                             var posParent = getOffsetRect(target.parentNode);
                                             var posTarget = getOffsetRect(target);
                                             var path = target.parentNode.getElementsByClassName('line')[0];
                                             var pathGradientId = path.getAttribute('fill').replace('url(#', '').replace(')', '');
                                             var toolTip = target.parentNode.lastChild;
                                             toolTip.setAttribute('style', '');
                                             toolTip.style.display = 'none';
 
                                             var currentPositionX = 0;
                                             var currentPositionY = 0;
                                             var currentI = 0;
                                             var idTarget = String(target.id).replace('Svg', '');
 
                                             for (var i = 0, size = arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'].length; i < size; i++) {
                                                 if (
                                                     (typeof arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][i + 1] === 'object' && arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][i]['x'] <= e.clientX - posTarget.left && e.clientX - posTarget.left >= arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][i + 1]['x'])
                                                 ) {
                                                     if (i === 0 || typeof arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][i + 1] !== 'object' || (e.clientX - posTarget.left) - arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][i]['x'] > (e.clientX - posTarget.left) - arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][i + 1]['x']) {
                                                         currentI = i;
                                                     } else {
                                                         currentI = i - 1;
                                                     }
 
                                                     currentPositionX = arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][currentI]['x'];
                                                     currentPositionY = arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][currentI]['y'];
                                                     var gradient = document.getElementById(pathGradientId);
                                                     var posGradient = 100 - Math.ceil((path.getBoundingClientRect().height - 6 - arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][currentI]['y']) / (path.getBoundingClientRect().height / 100));
                                                     gradient.childNodes[1].setAttribute('offset', posGradient + '%');
                                                     toolTip.children[1].innerHTML = arrayQuotesAssoc[idTarget]['graph'][currentI]['close'];
 
                                                     toolTip.style.left = (posTarget.left - posParent.left + 5 + currentPositionX) + 'px';
                                                     toolTip.style.top = (posTarget.top - posParent.top - 12 + currentPositionY) + 'px';
 
                                                     this.getElementsByClassName('line')[0].setAttribute('transform', 'translate(' + currentPositionX + ')');
                                                     this.getElementsByClassName('pointerLine')[0].setAttribute('transform', 'translate(' + String(currentPositionX) + ', '+ String(currentPositionY) +')');
 
                                                     timeoutIdToolTip = setTimeout(showTooltip, 300, toolTip);
                                                     break;
                                                 }
                                             }
                                         }
 
                                         target = target.parentNode;
                                     }
 
                                     target.onmouseleave = function (e) {
                                         e.target.getElementsByTagName('SPAN')[0].setAttribute('style', '');
                                         clearTimeout(timeoutIdToolTip);
                                     };
                                 };
                             } else {
                                 xmlHttp.abort();
                                 setTimeout(function () {
                                     xmlHttp.open("GET", "https://client-api.instaforex.com/api/chart/GetJsonCandles?symbol=" + encodeURIComponent(symbol.toUpperCase()) + "&type=M1&count=" + pointsOnGraph);
                                     xmlHttp.timeout = 1000;
                                     xmlHttp.send();
                                 }, 5000);
                             }
                         }
                     }, 100);
 
                     xmlHttp.send();
                 }
 
                 function showTooltip(target) {
                     target.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                     setTimeout(function () {
                         var style = getComputedStyle(target);
                         var style2 = getComputedStyle(target.getElementsByTagName('span')[0]);
 
                         if (target.parentNode.clientWidth < Math.ceil(parseFloat(style.width) + parseFloat(style.left))) {
                             target.setAttribute('class', 'reverse');
                             target.style.marginLeft = (-(Math.ceil(parseFloat(style.width) + parseFloat(style2.paddingLeft) + parseFloat(style2.paddingRight)))) + 'px';
                         } else {
                             target.setAttribute('class', '');
                         }
                         target.style.opacity = 1;
                         target.style.transform = 'scale(1,1)';
                     }, 50);
                 }
 
                 function setQuotesOneInfo(target, symbol) {
                     var i, size, difY, onePercentY, xPos, yPos;
                     if (!arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'].length) {
                         for (i = 0, size = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'].length; i < size; i++) {
                             if (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'] > arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i].Close || arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'] === 0) {
                                 arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i].Close;
                             }
 
                             if (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] < arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i].Close) {
                                 arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i].Close;
                             }
                         }
 
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['middle'] = (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min']) / 2 + arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'];
 
                         difY = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'];
                         onePercentY = difY / 100;
 
                         for (i = 0, size = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'].length; i < size; i++) {
                             xPos = 0;
                             yPos = 0;
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i] = [];
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['timestamp'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i]['Timestamp'];
                             if (i === 0) {
                                 xPos = svgWidth;
                             } else {
                                 difX = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i - 1]['Timestamp'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i]['Timestamp'];
                                 xPos = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i - 1]['x'] - Math.ceil((widthPoint / 100) * (difX / (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] / 100)));
                             }
 
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['close'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i]['Close'];
                             yPos = 100 - Math.ceil((difY - (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['initArray'][i]['Close'])) / onePercentY);
                             yPos = Math.ceil((svgHeight - 20) / 100 * yPos + 10);
                             if (isNaN(xPos) || isNaN(yPos)) { return; }
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['x'] = xPos;
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['y'] = yPos;
                         }
                     } else {
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'].unshift({
                             timestamp: arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['lastBuffer']['timestamp'],
                             x: 0,
                             close: arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['lastBuffer']['bid'],
                             y: 0
                         });
 
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'] = 0;
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] = 0;
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] = 0;
                         for (i = 0, size = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'].length; i < size; i++) {
                             if (typeof arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i + 1] === 'object') {
                                 arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] += (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i].timestamp - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i + 1].timestamp);
                             }
 
                             if (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['x'] >= 0) {
                                 if (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'] > arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i].close || arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'] === 0) {
                                     arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i].close;
                                 }
 
                                 if (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] < arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i].close) {
                                     arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i].close;
                                 }
                             } else {
                                 var tmpTime = Math.ceil(new Date().getTime() / 1000 + correctionTime - pointsOnGraph * secondPerOnePoint - secondPerOnePoint);
                                 while (tmpTime > arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'].length - 1].timestamp) {
                                     arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'].pop();
                                 }
                                 break;
                             }
                         }
 
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['middle'] = (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min']) / 2 + arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'];
 
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] = Math.ceil(arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] / i);
                         if (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] > secondPerOnePoint) {
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] = secondPerOnePoint;
                         }
 
                         difY = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['min'];
                         onePercentY = difY / 100;
 
                         for (i = 0, size = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'].length; i < size; i++) {
                             xPos = 0;
                             yPos = 0;
                             if (i === 0) {
                                 xPos = svgWidth;
                             } else {
                                 var difX = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i - 1]['timestamp'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['timestamp'];
                                 xPos = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i - 1]['x'] - Math.ceil((widthPoint / 100) * (difX / (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['secondPerOnePoint'] / 100)));
                             }
 
                             yPos = 100 - Math.ceil((difY - (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['max'] - arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['close'])) / onePercentY);
                             yPos = Math.ceil((svgHeight - 20) / 100 * yPos + 10);
                             if (isNaN(xPos) || isNaN(yPos)) { return; }
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['x'] = xPos;
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['y'] = yPos;
                         }
                     }
 
                     var pathD = 'M-4 ' + (svgHeight + 6) + ' ';
 
                     for (i = arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'].length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                         if (typeof arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i - 1] !== 'object' || arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i - 1]['x'] >= 0) {
                             pathD += 'L ' + arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['x'] + ' ' + (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][i]['y']) + ' ';
                         }
                     }
 
                     pathD += 'L ' + (svgWidth + 6) + ' ' + (arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['graph'][0]['y']) + ' ';
                     pathD += 'L ' + (svgWidth + 6) + ' ' + (svgHeight + 6) + ' ';
 
                     document.getElementById(symbol + 'Svg').getElementsByTagName('path')[0].setAttribute('d', pathD);
 
                     pathD += 'z';
 
                     var path = document.getElementById(symbol + 'Svg').getElementsByTagName('path')[1];
                     path.setAttribute('d', pathD);
                     path.setAttribute('stroke', arrayColor[arrayQuotesAssoc[symbol]['color']]);
                     path.setAttribute('stroke-width', '1');
 
                     checkReadyQuotes[symbol] = true;
                 }
 
                 function getXmlHttp() {
                     var xmlhttp;
                     try {
                         xmlhttp = new ActiveXObject("Msxml2.XMLHTTP");
                     } catch (e) {
                         try {
                             xmlhttp = new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP");
                         } catch (E) {
                             xmlhttp = false;
                         }
                     }
                     if (!xmlhttp && typeof(XMLHttpRequest) === 'function') {
                         xmlhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
                     }
                     return xmlhttp;
                 }
 
                 function getQuotesFromSocket() {
                     var arrayQuotesTmp = arrayQuotes;
                     arrayQuotesTmp.map(function (quote, index) {
                         arrayQuotesTmp[index] = (typeof arrayKostiley[arrayQuotesTmp[index].toLowerCase()] === 'string') ? arrayKostiley[arrayQuotesTmp[index].toLowerCase()] : arrayQuotesTmp[index].toUpperCase();
                     });
                     socket = io.connect('https://qrtm1.ifxdb.com:8443');
 
                     socket.on('connect', function () {
                         socket.emit('subscribe', arrayQuotesTmp, 'standard');
 
                         intervalIdQuotesFromSocket = setInterval(function () {
                             setQuotesFromSocket();
                         }, timeoutBuffer);
                     });
                     socket.on('quotes', function (data) {
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[String(data.msg.symbol).toLowerCase()].buffer.bid = data.msg.bid;
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[String(data.msg.symbol).toLowerCase()].buffer.change = parseFloat((parseFloat(arrayQuotesAssoc[String(data.msg.symbol).toLowerCase()].buffer.change) + parseFloat(data.msg.change)).toFixed(data.msg.digits));
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[String(data.msg.symbol).toLowerCase()].buffer.timestamp = data.msg.lasttime;
                         arrayQuotesAssoc[String(data.msg.symbol).toLowerCase()].buffer.digits = data.msg.digits;
                     });
                 }
 
                 function setQuotesFromSocket() {
                     Object.keys(arrayQuotesAssoc).map(function (quote, index) {
                         if (arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['buffer']['change'] !== 0 && arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['buffer']['timestamp'] !== 0) {
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['lastBuffer']['bid'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['buffer']['bid'];
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['lastBuffer']['change'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['buffer']['change'];
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['lastBuffer']['digits'] = arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['buffer']['digits'];
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['lastBuffer']['timestamp'] = Math.ceil(new Date().getTime() / 1000 + correctionTime);
 
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['buffer']['change'] = 0;
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['buffer']['timestamp'] = 0;
 
                             var target = document.getElementById(quote);
 
                             target.children[0].children[1].innerHTML = arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['lastBuffer']['bid'];
                             arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].change24h = parseFloat((parseFloat(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].change24h) + parseFloat(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['lastBuffer']['change'])).toFixed(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote]['lastBuffer']['digits']));
 
                             if (arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].change24h < 0) {
                                 target.children[0].children[2].setAttribute('class', 'minus');
                                 target.children[0].children[2].innerHTML = '(-' + ((Math.abs(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].change24h) / (arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].bid / 100)).toFixed(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].digits)) + '%)';
                             } else {
                                 target.children[0].children[2].setAttribute('class', 'plus');
                                 target.children[0].children[2].innerHTML = '(+' + ((Math.abs(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].change24h) / (arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].bid / 100)).toFixed(arrayQuotesAssoc[quote].digits)) + '%)';
                             }
 
                             setQuotesOneInfo(target, quote);
                         }
                     });
                 }
 
                 function getOffsetRect(elem) {
                     if (typeof (elem) !== 'object') {
                         return;
                     }
                     var box = elem.getBoundingClientRect();
 
                     var body = document.body;
                     var docElem = document.documentElement;
 
                     var scrollTop = window.pageYOffset || docElem.scrollTop || body.scrollTop;
                     var scrollLeft = window.pageXOffset || docElem.scrollLeft || body.scrollLeft;
 
                     var clientTop = docElem.clientTop || body.clientTop || 0;
                     var clientLeft = docElem.clientLeft || body.clientLeft || 0;
 
                     var top = box.top + scrollTop - clientTop;
                     var left = box.left + scrollLeft - clientLeft;
 
                     return {top: Math.round(top), left: Math.round(left)}
                 }
 
                 function onBlur() {
                  if(socket){ socket.close();}
                 }
                 function onFocus(){
                     prepareGraph();
                 }
             })(io);
         }
     }, 50);
 </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    $('.slider1').each(function() {
  var $this = $(this);
  var $group = $this.find('.slide_group');
  var $slides = $this.find('.slide');
  var bulletArray = [];
  var currentIndex = 0;
  var timeout;
  
  function move(newIndex) {
    var animateLeft, slideLeft;
    
    advance();
    
    if ($group.is(':animated') || currentIndex === newIndex) {
      return;
    }
    
    bulletArray[currentIndex].removeClass('active');
    bulletArray[newIndex].addClass('active');
    
    if (newIndex > currentIndex) {
      slideLeft = '100%';
      animateLeft = '-100%';
    } else {
      slideLeft = '-100%';
      animateLeft = '100%';
    }
    
    $slides.eq(newIndex).css({
      display: 'block',
      left: slideLeft
    });
    $group.animate({
      left: animateLeft
    }, function() {
      $slides.eq(currentIndex).css({
        display: 'none'
      });
      $slides.eq(newIndex).css({
        left: 0
      });
      $group.css({
        left: 0
      });
      currentIndex = newIndex;
    });
  }
  
  function advance() {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    timeout = setTimeout(function() {
      if (currentIndex < ($slides.length - 1)) {
        move(currentIndex + 1);
      } else {
        move(0);
      }
    }, 5000);
  }
  
  $('.next_btn').on('click', function() {
    if (currentIndex < ($slides.length - 1)) {
      move(currentIndex + 1);
    } else {
      move(0);
    }
  });
  
  $('.previous_btn').on('click', function() {
    if (currentIndex !== 0) {
      move(currentIndex - 1);
    } else {
      move(3);
    }
  });
  
  $.each($slides, function(index) {
    var $button = $('<a class="slide_btn">&bull;</a>');
    
    if (index === currentIndex) {
      $button.addClass('active');
    }
    $button.on('click', function() {
      move(index);
    }).appendTo('.slide_buttons');
    bulletArray.push($button);
  });
  
  advance();
})

</script>

	$(window).load(function(){
		if($('html')[0].lang=='hi'){
			var ffifcp = $('#_bbtitle_ffifcp');

		function Cbnr(objName){
			this.obj = objName;
			this.aVSas = [];
			this.currentCbnr = 0;
		}
		Cbnr.prototype.bnr = function(bnrType, bnrPath, bnrDuration, bnrHeight, bnrWidth, bnrURL, bnrTitle, bnrID) {
			this.aVSas[this.aVSas.length] = new VSa(this.obj +"_"+ this.aVSas.length, bnrType, bnrPath, bnrDuration, bnrHeight, bnrWidth, bnrURL, bnrTitle, bnrID);
		}
		function VSa(bnrName, bnrType, bnrPath, bnrDuration, bnrHeight, bnrWidth, bnrURL, bnrTitle, bnrID) {
			this.bnrName = bnrName;
			this.bnrType = bnrType;
			this.bnrPath = bnrPath;
			this.bnrDuration = bnrDuration;
			this.bnrHeight = bnrHeight;
			this.bnrWidth = bnrWidth;
			this.bnrURL= bnrURL;
			this.bnrTitle= bnrTitle;
			this.bnrID = bnrID;
		}
		Cbnr.prototype.toString = function() {
			bannerinfo = "";
			for (var iCtr=0; iCtr < this.aVSas.length; iCtr++)
			{
				bannerinfo = bannerinfo + '<div title="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrTitle+'" id="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrID+'" style="display:none;vertical-align:middle;cursor:pointer;">';
				if (this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrType == "FLASH")
				{
					bannerinfo = bannerinfo + '<div style="z-index:0"><object classid="clsid:D27CDB6E-AE6D-11cf-96B8-444553540000" codebase="http://download.macromedia.com/pub/shockwave/cabs/flash/swflash.cab#version=6,0,0,0" width="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrWidth+'" height="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrHeight+'" title="bnr_'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrName+'" id="bnr_'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrID+'" ><param name="allowScriptAccess" value="sameDomain" /><param name="flashvars" value="clickTAG='+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrURL+'" /><param name="movie" value="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrPath+'" /><param name="quality" value="high" /><param name="wmode" value="opaque" /><param name="bgcolor" value="FFFFFF"><embed src="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrPath+'" quality="high" width="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrWidth+'" height="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrHeight+'" type="application/x-shockwave-flash" pluginspage="http://www.macromedia.com/go/getflashplayer" allowScriptAccess="sameDomain" flashvars="clickTAG='+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrURL+'" wmode="opaque" bgcolor="FFFFFF" ></embed></object></div><div style="width:'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrWidth+'px;height:'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrHeight+';z-index:99;top:0;overflow:hidden;position:absolute;display:block;" onclick="javascript:document.vsab_goto.submit();"><img width="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrWidth+'" height="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrHeight+'" src="images/misc/banner.png" /></div>';
				}
				else if (this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrType == "IMAGE")
				{
					bannerinfo = bannerinfo + '<img src="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrPath+'" border="0" alt="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrTitle+'" height="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrHeight+'" width="'+this.aVSas[iCtr].bnrWidth+'" />';
				}
				bannerinfo = bannerinfo + '</div>';
			}
			return bannerinfo;
		}
		Cbnr.prototype.start = function() {
			this.changeCbnr();
			thisCbnrObj = this.obj;
			if (this.currentCbnr == 0)
			{
				cbanner = this.currentCbnr;
			}
			else
			{
				cbanner = this.currentCbnr-1;
			}
			setTimeout(thisCbnrObj+".start()", this.aVSas[cbanner].bnrDuration * 1000);
		}

		Cbnr.prototype.changeCbnr = function(){
			thisCbnr = '';
			prevCbnr = -1;
			if (this.currentCbnr < this.aVSas.length)
			{
				thisCbnr = this.currentCbnr;
				if (this.aVSas.length > 1)
				{
					if (thisCbnr > 0)
					{
						prevCbnr = thisCbnr - 1;
					}
					else
					{
						prevCbnr = this.aVSas.length-1;
					}
				}
				if (this.currentCbnr < this.aVSas.length - 1)
				{
					this.currentCbnr = this.currentCbnr + 1;
				}
				else
				{
					this.currentCbnr = 0;
				}
			}
			if (prevCbnr >= 0)
			{
				document.getElementById(this.aVSas[prevCbnr].bnrID).style.display="none";
			}
			document.vsab_goto.id.value = this.aVSas[thisCbnr].bnrID;
			document.vsab_goto.url.value = this.aVSas[thisCbnr].bnrURL;
			document.vsab_goto.name.value = this.aVSas[thisCbnr].bnrTitle;
			document.getElementById(this.aVSas[thisCbnr].bnrID).style.display="block";
		}
	</script>

function myFunction(id_no) {

    var id_no = id_no;
    var x = document.getElementById("raashid_div_id"+id_no);
    var y = document.getElementById("raashid_div_h2_id"+id_no);

    if (x.style.display === "none") {
        x.style.display = "block";
        y.style.display = "block";

    } else {
        x.style.display = "none";
        y.style.display = "none";

    }
}

function myFunction(id_no) {

    var id_no = id_no;
    var x = document.getElementById("raashid_div_id"+id_no);
    var y = document.getElementById("raashid_div_h2_id"+id_no);

    if (x.style.display === "none") {
        x.style.display = "block";
        y.style.display = "block";

    } else {
        x.style.display = "none";
        y.style.display = "none";

    }
}


</script>

<script>
function myFunction(id_no) {

    var id_no = id_no;
    var x = document.getElementById("raashid_div_id"+id_no);
    var y = document.getElementById("raashid_div_h2_id"+id_no);

    if (x.style.display === "none") {
        x.style.display = "block";
        y.style.display = "block";

    } else {
        x.style.display = "none";
        y.style.display = "none";

    }
}


</script>

function myFunction(id_no) {

    var id_no = id_no;
    var x = document.getElementById("raashid_div_id"+id_no);
    var y = document.getElementById("raashid_div_h2_id"+id_no);

    if (x.style.display === "none") {
        x.style.display = "block";
        y.style.display = "block";

    } else {
        x.style.display = "none";
        y.style.display = "none";

    }
}

<script>
    $(document).ready(function() {
        $("#collapse_imdmstat_img").click(function() {
             

            if ($("#imdmstat").is(":visible")) {
                $('#collapse_imdmstat_img').attr("src", "/images/metro/collapse_40b_collapsed.png");
                $("#imdmstat").hide();
            } else {
                $('#collapse_imdmstat_img').attr("src", "/images/metro/collapse_40b.png");
                $("#imdmstat").show();
            }


        });
    });
</script>

<script>
 
$(document).ready(function() {
    $("#apealtoggle").click(function(e) {
        $(".apeal1").toggle();
        e.stopPropagation();
    });

    $(document).click(function(e) {
        if (!$(e.target).is('.apeal1, .apeal1 *')) {
            $(".apeal1").hide();
        }
    });
});
</script>

<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
var oldEvt = window.onload; 
window.onload = function() 
{
	if (oldEvt) oldEvt(); 
	auto_reload_news_frame(); 
	if(navigator.appName=="Microsoft Internet Explorer"&& navigator.appVersion.indexOf("MSIE 8.")==-1&& navigator.appVersion.indexOf("MSIE 9.")==-1) 		
		document.getElementById("wgo_onlineusers_list").innerHTML=document.getElementById("wgo_onlineusers_list").innerHTML.replace(/<\/a>/gi,"</a>,");
	
} 
var height_nw,kol_vo_news;
var nw_flag=0;
function auto_reload_news_frame()
{
	height_nw=document.getElementById("content_container").clientHeight-document.getElementById('sidebar_container').clientHeight;
	kol_vo_news=Math.round(height_nw/135)+5;
	document.getElementById("mt5iframe_news_analytics").src=document.getElementById("mt5iframe_news_analytics").src.replace(/&count=(\d+)&/,"&count="+kol_vo_news+"&");
document.getElementById("mt5iframe_news_analytics").height = (82+135*kol_vo_news)+'px';
}
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
$(document).ready(function() {

  // If the 'hide cookie is not set we show the message
  if (!readCookie('hide')) {
    $('.iframes').show().css('display', 'flex');
  }

  // Add the event that closes the popup and sets the cookie that tells us to
  // not show it again until one day has passed.
  $('.close_iframe').click(function() {
    $('.iframes').hide();
    createCookie('hide', true, 1)
    return false;
  });

});

// ---
// And some generic cookie logic
// ---
function createCookie(name,value,days) {
  if (days) {
    var date = new Date();
    date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days*24*60*60*1000));
    var expires = "; expires="+date.toGMTString();
  }
  else var expires = "";
  document.cookie = name+"="+value+expires+"; path=/";
}

function readCookie(name) {
  var nameEQ = name + "=";
  var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
  for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
    var c = ca[i];
    while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
    if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length,c.length);
  }
  return null;
}

function eraseCookie(name) {
  createCookie(name,"",-1);
}


    
</script>
